import { BlockInfoCard } from "@/components/ui/block-info-card"

<BlockInfoCard type="rootly" color="#6C72C8" />

Nutzungsanweisungen [#nutzungsanweisungen]

Integrate Rootly incident management into workflows. Create and manage incidents, alerts, services, severities, and retrospectives.

Tools [#tools]

rootly_create_incident [#rootly_create_incident]

Create a new incident in Rootly with optional severity, services, and teams.

Eingabe [#eingabe]

| Parameter          | Typ     | Erforderlich | Beschreibung                                                                                                                              |
| ------------------ | ------- | ------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `apiKey`           | string  | Ja           | Rootly API key                                                                                                                            |
| `title`            | string  | Nein         | The title of the incident (auto-generated if not provided)                                                                                |
| `summary`          | string  | Nein         | A summary of the incident                                                                                                                 |
| `severityId`       | string  | Nein         | Severity ID to attach to the incident                                                                                                     |
| `status`           | string  | Nein         | Incident status (in\_triage, started, detected, acknowledged, mitigated, resolved, closed, cancelled, scheduled, in\_progress, completed) |
| `kind`             | string  | Nein         | Incident kind (normal, normal\_sub, test, test\_sub, example, example\_sub, backfilled, scheduled, scheduled\_sub)                        |
| `serviceIds`       | string  | Nein         | Comma-separated service IDs to attach                                                                                                     |
| `environmentIds`   | string  | Nein         | Comma-separated environment IDs to attach                                                                                                 |
| `groupIds`         | string  | Nein         | Comma-separated team/group IDs to attach                                                                                                  |
| `incidentTypeIds`  | string  | Nein         | Comma-separated incident type IDs to attach                                                                                               |
| `functionalityIds` | string  | Nein         | Comma-separated functionality IDs to attach                                                                                               |
| `labels`           | string  | Nein         | Labels as JSON object, e.g. \{"platform":"osx","version":"1.29"}                                                                          |
| `private`          | boolean | Nein         | Create as a private incident (cannot be undone)                                                                                           |

Ausgabe [#ausgabe]

| Parameter        | Typ     | Beschreibung                    |
| ---------------- | ------- | ------------------------------- |
| `incident`       | object  | The created incident            |
| ↳ `id`           | string  | Unique incident ID              |
| ↳ `sequentialId` | number  | Sequential incident number      |
| ↳ `title`        | string  | Incident title                  |
| ↳ `slug`         | string  | Incident slug                   |
| ↳ `kind`         | string  | Incident kind                   |
| ↳ `summary`      | string  | Incident summary                |
| ↳ `status`       | string  | Incident status                 |
| ↳ `private`      | boolean | Whether the incident is private |
| ↳ `url`          | string  | URL to the incident             |
| ↳ `shortUrl`     | string  | Short URL to the incident       |
| ↳ `severityName` | string  | Severity name                   |
| ↳ `severityId`   | string  | Severity ID                     |
| ↳ `createdAt`    | string  | Creation date                   |
| ↳ `updatedAt`    | string  | Last update date                |
| ↳ `startedAt`    | string  | Start date                      |
| ↳ `mitigatedAt`  | string  | Mitigation date                 |
| ↳ `resolvedAt`   | string  | Resolution date                 |
| ↳ `closedAt`     | string  | Closed date                     |

rootly_get_incident [#rootly_get_incident]

Retrieve a single incident by ID from Rootly.

Eingabe [#eingabe-1]

| Parameter    | Typ    | Erforderlich | Beschreibung                       |
| ------------ | ------ | ------------ | ---------------------------------- |
| `apiKey`     | string | Ja           | Rootly API key                     |
| `incidentId` | string | Ja           | The ID of the incident to retrieve |

Ausgabe [#ausgabe-1]

| Parameter        | Typ     | Beschreibung                    |
| ---------------- | ------- | ------------------------------- |
| `incident`       | object  | The incident details            |
| ↳ `id`           | string  | Unique incident ID              |
| ↳ `sequentialId` | number  | Sequential incident number      |
| ↳ `title`        | string  | Incident title                  |
| ↳ `slug`         | string  | Incident slug                   |
| ↳ `kind`         | string  | Incident kind                   |
| ↳ `summary`      | string  | Incident summary                |
| ↳ `status`       | string  | Incident status                 |
| ↳ `private`      | boolean | Whether the incident is private |
| ↳ `url`          | string  | URL to the incident             |
| ↳ `shortUrl`     | string  | Short URL to the incident       |
| ↳ `severityName` | string  | Severity name                   |
| ↳ `severityId`   | string  | Severity ID                     |
| ↳ `createdAt`    | string  | Creation date                   |
| ↳ `updatedAt`    | string  | Last update date                |
| ↳ `startedAt`    | string  | Start date                      |
| ↳ `mitigatedAt`  | string  | Mitigation date                 |
| ↳ `resolvedAt`   | string  | Resolution date                 |
| ↳ `closedAt`     | string  | Closed date                     |

rootly_update_incident [#rootly_update_incident]

Update an existing incident in Rootly (status, severity, summary, etc.).

Eingabe [#eingabe-2]

| Parameter             | Typ     | Erforderlich | Beschreibung                                                                                                                             |
| --------------------- | ------- | ------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `apiKey`              | string  | Ja           | Rootly API key                                                                                                                           |
| `incidentId`          | string  | Ja           | The ID of the incident to update                                                                                                         |
| `title`               | string  | Nein         | Updated incident title                                                                                                                   |
| `summary`             | string  | Nein         | Updated incident summary                                                                                                                 |
| `severityId`          | string  | Nein         | Updated severity ID                                                                                                                      |
| `status`              | string  | Nein         | Updated status (in\_triage, started, detected, acknowledged, mitigated, resolved, closed, cancelled, scheduled, in\_progress, completed) |
| `kind`                | string  | Nein         | Incident kind (normal, normal\_sub, test, test\_sub, example, example\_sub, backfilled, scheduled, scheduled\_sub)                       |
| `private`             | boolean | Nein         | Set incident as private (cannot be undone)                                                                                               |
| `serviceIds`          | string  | Nein         | Comma-separated service IDs                                                                                                              |
| `environmentIds`      | string  | Nein         | Comma-separated environment IDs                                                                                                          |
| `groupIds`            | string  | Nein         | Comma-separated team/group IDs                                                                                                           |
| `incidentTypeIds`     | string  | Nein         | Comma-separated incident type IDs to attach                                                                                              |
| `functionalityIds`    | string  | Nein         | Comma-separated functionality IDs to attach                                                                                              |
| `labels`              | string  | Nein         | Labels as JSON object, e.g. \{"platform":"osx","version":"1.29"}                                                                         |
| `mitigationMessage`   | string  | Nein         | How was the incident mitigated?                                                                                                          |
| `resolutionMessage`   | string  | Nein         | How was the incident resolved?                                                                                                           |
| `cancellationMessage` | string  | Nein         | Why was the incident cancelled?                                                                                                          |

Ausgabe [#ausgabe-2]

| Parameter        | Typ     | Beschreibung                    |
| ---------------- | ------- | ------------------------------- |
| `incident`       | object  | The updated incident            |
| ↳ `id`           | string  | Unique incident ID              |
| ↳ `sequentialId` | number  | Sequential incident number      |
| ↳ `title`        | string  | Incident title                  |
| ↳ `slug`         | string  | Incident slug                   |
| ↳ `kind`         | string  | Incident kind                   |
| ↳ `summary`      | string  | Incident summary                |
| ↳ `status`       | string  | Incident status                 |
| ↳ `private`      | boolean | Whether the incident is private |
| ↳ `url`          | string  | URL to the incident             |
| ↳ `shortUrl`     | string  | Short URL to the incident       |
| ↳ `severityName` | string  | Severity name                   |
| ↳ `severityId`   | string  | Severity ID                     |
| ↳ `createdAt`    | string  | Creation date                   |
| ↳ `updatedAt`    | string  | Last update date                |
| ↳ `startedAt`    | string  | Start date                      |
| ↳ `mitigatedAt`  | string  | Mitigation date                 |
| ↳ `resolvedAt`   | string  | Resolution date                 |
| ↳ `closedAt`     | string  | Closed date                     |

rootly_list_incidents [#rootly_list_incidents]

List incidents from Rootly with optional filtering by status, severity, and more.

Eingabe [#eingabe-3]

| Parameter      | Typ    | Erforderlich | Beschreibung                                                                                                                               |
| -------------- | ------ | ------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `apiKey`       | string | Ja           | Rootly API key                                                                                                                             |
| `status`       | string | Nein         | Filter by status (in\_triage, started, detected, acknowledged, mitigated, resolved, closed, cancelled, scheduled, in\_progress, completed) |
| `severity`     | string | Nein         | Filter by severity slug                                                                                                                    |
| `search`       | string | Nein         | Search term to filter incidents                                                                                                            |
| `services`     | string | Nein         | Filter by service slugs (comma-separated)                                                                                                  |
| `teams`        | string | Nein         | Filter by team slugs (comma-separated)                                                                                                     |
| `environments` | string | Nein         | Filter by environment slugs (comma-separated)                                                                                              |
| `sort`         | string | Nein         | Sort order (e.g., -created\_at, created\_at, -started\_at)                                                                                 |
| `pageSize`     | number | Nein         | Number of items per page (default: 20)                                                                                                     |
| `pageNumber`   | number | Nein         | Page number for pagination                                                                                                                 |

Ausgabe [#ausgabe-3]

| Parameter        | Typ     | Beschreibung                       |
| ---------------- | ------- | ---------------------------------- |
| `incidents`      | array   | List of incidents                  |
| ↳ `id`           | string  | Unique incident ID                 |
| ↳ `sequentialId` | number  | Sequential incident number         |
| ↳ `title`        | string  | Incident title                     |
| ↳ `slug`         | string  | Incident slug                      |
| ↳ `kind`         | string  | Incident kind                      |
| ↳ `summary`      | string  | Incident summary                   |
| ↳ `status`       | string  | Incident status                    |
| ↳ `private`      | boolean | Whether the incident is private    |
| ↳ `url`          | string  | URL to the incident                |
| ↳ `shortUrl`     | string  | Short URL to the incident          |
| ↳ `severityName` | string  | Severity name                      |
| ↳ `severityId`   | string  | Severity ID                        |
| ↳ `createdAt`    | string  | Creation date                      |
| ↳ `updatedAt`    | string  | Last update date                   |
| ↳ `startedAt`    | string  | Start date                         |
| ↳ `mitigatedAt`  | string  | Mitigation date                    |
| ↳ `resolvedAt`   | string  | Resolution date                    |
| ↳ `closedAt`     | string  | Closed date                        |
| `totalCount`     | number  | Total number of incidents returned |

rootly_create_alert [#rootly_create_alert]

Create a new alert in Rootly for on-call notification and routing.

Eingabe [#eingabe-4]

| Parameter          | Typ    | Erforderlich | Beschreibung                                                            |
| ------------------ | ------ | ------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `apiKey`           | string | Ja           | Rootly API key                                                          |
| `summary`          | string | Ja           | The summary of the alert                                                |
| `description`      | string | Nein         | A detailed description of the alert                                     |
| `source`           | string | Ja           | The source of the alert (e.g., api, manual, datadog, pagerduty)         |
| `status`           | string | Nein         | Alert status on creation (open, triggered)                              |
| `serviceIds`       | string | Nein         | Comma-separated service IDs to attach                                   |
| `groupIds`         | string | Nein         | Comma-separated team/group IDs to attach                                |
| `environmentIds`   | string | Nein         | Comma-separated environment IDs to attach                               |
| `externalId`       | string | Nein         | External ID for the alert                                               |
| `externalUrl`      | string | Nein         | External URL for the alert                                              |
| `deduplicationKey` | string | Nein         | Alerts sharing the same deduplication key are treated as a single alert |

Ausgabe [#ausgabe-4]

| Parameter            | Typ    | Beschreibung      |
| -------------------- | ------ | ----------------- |
| `alert`              | object | The created alert |
| ↳ `id`               | string | Unique alert ID   |
| ↳ `shortId`          | string | Short alert ID    |
| ↳ `summary`          | string | Alert summary     |
| ↳ `description`      | string | Alert description |
| ↳ `source`           | string | Alert source      |
| ↳ `status`           | string | Alert status      |
| ↳ `externalId`       | string | External ID       |
| ↳ `externalUrl`      | string | External URL      |
| ↳ `deduplicationKey` | string | Deduplication key |
| ↳ `createdAt`        | string | Creation date     |
| ↳ `updatedAt`        | string | Last update date  |
| ↳ `startedAt`        | string | Start date        |
| ↳ `endedAt`          | string | End date          |

rootly_add_incident_event [#rootly_add_incident_event]

Add a timeline event to an existing incident in Rootly.

Eingabe [#eingabe-6]

| Parameter    | Typ    | Erforderlich | Beschreibung                               |
| ------------ | ------ | ------------ | ------------------------------------------ |
| `apiKey`     | string | Ja           | Rootly API key                             |
| `incidentId` | string | Ja           | The ID of the incident to add the event to |
| `event`      | string | Ja           | The summary/description of the event       |
| `visibility` | string | Nein         | Event visibility (internal or external)    |
